This release 1453 added, 11 changed, 1992 removed, 3 deprecated API endpoint(s).

:::danger Breaking change
This release removes endpoints or adds newly-required request fields. Review the **Removed** and **Changed** sections before upgrading your integration.
:::

<!-- truncate -->

## Changed

- `DELETE /api/custom-fields/{customField}` — Delete Custom Field
  - new parameter(s): `custom_field`
- `DELETE /api/discount-codes/{discountCode}` — Delete Discount Code
  - new parameter(s): `discount_code`
- `GET /api/discount-codes/{discountCode}` — Get Discount Code
  - new parameter(s): `discount_code`
- `GET /api/products/search` — Search Products
  - new parameter(s): `domain`
- `GET /api/sales-reps` — List Sales Reps
  - new parameter(s): `domain`
- `GET /api/sub-sales-channels` — List Sub Sales Channels **(breaking)**
  - new parameter(s): `domain`
  - removed parameter(s): `Content-Type`, `page`, `per_page`
- `POST /api/shipment-trackings/backfill` — Backfill Shipment Trackings
  - new response code(s): `422`
- `POST /api/shipment-trackings/register` — Register Shipment Tracking
  - removed response code(s): `404`
- `POST /webhooks/seventeentrack/{webhook_token}` — 17TRACK Webhook (TRACKING_UPDATED) **(breaking)**
  - new parameter(s): `Accept`
  - removed parameter(s): `sign`
  - removed response code(s): `401`, `404`
- `PUT /api/custom-fields/{customField}` — Update Custom Field
  - new parameter(s): `custom_field`
- `PUT /api/discount-codes/{discountCode}` — Update Discount Code
  - new parameter(s): `discount_code`

- `GET /api/faire/inventory` — List Faire Inventory

- `POST /api/faire/inventory/push` — Push Inventory to Faire
